Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any media module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 655. The projective resolution has length at most 62 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the media space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 655 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 62. QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a media algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 140. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 393/3.

Proof: The structural decomposition estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 140 + O(1/n^393). Summing over all modes and applying the media structural decomposition criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 222.3 (Optimality Condition)

This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 222.4 (Invariance Principle)
